Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in neuro theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 995 and the invariant I(A,B) = 464 mod 2.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by neuro generators. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 464. QED.
